```text
"""Auto-derive the "current state" facts block for report.md from registry.

Problem being solved: report.md was a free-form narrative Director wrote by
hand, and its "facts" section (what's running, GPU allocation, recent
completions) kept drifting from registry.yaml. On 2026-04-24 we observed
report.md say "seetacloud-westd cuda:0-7 all idle" while registry had two
RUNNING experiments on cuda:2/3 — because Director wrote that paragraph an
hour earlier and never refreshed it.

Fix: split report.md into an AUTO-generated facts block and a human-authored
analysis tail. Callers invoke ``auto_build_report_facts(project)`` to
regenerate the facts block in-place between marker comments:

    &lt;!-- AUTO-FACTS-BEGIN (generated ...) -->
    ...canonical facts derived from registry + resource tracker...
    &lt;!-- AUTO-FACTS-END -->
```

```text
def _get_connection_class():
    """Lazy-load Fabric Connection to avoid Paramiko init hang at import time."""
    global Connection
    if Connection is None:
        try:
            from fabric import Connection as _Conn
            Connection = _Conn
        except ImportError:
            pass
    return Connection


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Input validation — prevent shell injection in remote commands
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_RE_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-]+$")
_RE_PID = re.compile(r"^[0-9]+$")
_RE_SAFE_PATH = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_./~\-]+$")


--
    port: int = 22      # SSH port (non-standard for AutoDL)
    identity_file: str = ""  # path to SSH private key (e.g. Ed25519)


def _resolve_account(server: str, account: str | None = None) -> ResolvedAccount:
    """Resolve server + optional account to full SSH connection info.

    Format:
        servers:
          A6000:
            host: 10.60.200.102
            accounts:
              - user: sunzhen
                work_dir: ~/workspace
    """
    resources = _load_resources()
    srv = resources.get("servers", {}).get(server)
    if srv is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown server '{server}'. Available: {list(resources.get('servers', {}).keys())}")

    raw_host = srv["host"]
    accounts = srv.get("accounts")
    if not accounts:
        # Legacy format: host includes user (e.g. "caoxiaoyu@10.60.200.102")
        if "@" in raw_host:
            user, hostname = raw_host.split("@", 1)
        else:
            user, hostname = "root", raw_host
        accounts = [{"user": user, "work_dir": srv.get("work_dir", "~"), "env_activate": srv.get("env_activate", "")}]
    else:
        hostname = raw_host

    if account:
        matched = next((a for a in accounts if a["user"] == account), None)
        if matched is None:
            available = [a["user"] for a in accounts]
            raise ValueError(f"Account '{account}' not found on server '{server}'. Available: {available}")
    else:
```

```json
    "stdout": "def _get_connection_class():\n    \"\"\"Lazy-load Fabric Connection to avoid Paramiko init hang at import time.\"\"\"\n    global Connection\n    if Connection is None:\n        try:\n            from fabric import Connection as _Conn\n            Connection = _Conn\n        except ImportError:\n            pass\n    return Connection\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Input validation — prevent shell injection in remote commands\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n_RE_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r\"^[a-zA-Z0-9_\\-]+$\")\n_RE_PID = re.compile(r\"^[0-9]+$\")\n_RE_SAFE_PATH = re.compile(r\"^[a-zA-Z0-9_./~\\-]+$\")\n\n\n--\n    port: int = 22      # SSH port (non-standard for AutoDL)\n    identity_file: str = \"\"  # path to SSH private key (e.g. Ed25519)\n\n\ndef _resolve_account(server: str, account: str | None = None) -> ResolvedAccount:\n    \"\"\"Resolve server + optional account to full SSH connection info.\n\n    Format:\n        servers:\n          A6000:\n            host: 10.60.200.102\n            accounts:\n              - user: sunzhen\n                work_dir: ~/workspace\n    \"\"\"\n    resources = _load_resources()\n    srv = resources.get(\"servers\", {}).get(server)\n    if srv is None:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown server '{server}'. Available: {list(resources.get('servers', {}).keys())}\")\n\n    raw_host = srv[\"host\"]\n    accounts = srv.get(\"accounts\")\n    if not accounts:\n        # Legacy format: host includes user (e.g. \"caoxiaoyu@10.60.200.102\")\n        if \"@\" in raw_host:\n            user, hostname = raw_host.split(\"@\", 1)\n        else:\n            user, hostname = \"root\", raw_host\n        accounts = [{\"user\": user, \"work_dir\": srv.get(\"work_dir\", \"~\"), \"env_activate\": srv.get(\"env_activate\", \"\")}]\n    else:\n        hostname = raw_host\n\n    if account:\n        matched = next((a for a in accounts if a[\"user\"] == account), None)\n        if matched is None:\n            available = [a[\"user\"] for a in accounts]\n            raise ValueError(f\"Account '{account}' not found on server '{server}'. Available: {available}\")\n    else:",
```
